user
Ты ревьюишь волну 1 («Оболочка») проекта Plain — личного Markdown reader/editor для Windows (Tauri 2 + React 19 + TS + Vite). Ничего не правь — только читай и пиши отзыв.

Контекст: это личный проект для одного человека; ТЗ — файл `ТЗ-Plain.md` (норма), дизайн-токены — `research/00-design-system-from-mockups.md`. Волна 1 по §17 п.1: скаффолд, токены/тема/шрифт, полоса режимов, статус-бар, рейка, стор, хоткеи по KeyboardEvent.code, settings.json, single-instance, аргумент-путь. Код волны: `src/`, `src-tauri/` (без icons), `package.json`, `vite.config.ts`, `tsconfig.json`, `index.html`. Rust ещё НЕ компилировался (на машине не было MSVC) — на Rust-код смотри особенно внимательно: сверяй с API tauri 2.11 (исходники плагинов лежат в ~/.cargo/registry).

Что ищем, по приоритету:
1. Баги и несоответствия ТЗ (§3, §4, §10, §12, §13, §14, §15): неверная геометрия/токены, хоткеи, которые не сработают в русской раскладке, ошибки в capabilities/permissions Tauri, CSP, неправильное использование API плагинов, ошибки в Rust (single-instance, emit, fs scope).
2. Оверинжиниринг — абстракции «на будущее», лишние слои, лишние зависимости. Критерий: «окупится ли за месяц использования одним человеком».
3. То, что затруднит следующие волны (read-пайплайн, CM6-редактор, сохранность на Rust): модель стора, границы модулей.
4. Качество вёрстки относительно макета: `mockups/Markdown Editor.dc.html` (экраны 1a/1b/1c).

Формат ответа: список находок, каждая — файл:строка, суть, почему важно, предложение. Раздели на «критично / стоит поправить / вкусовщина». Не пересказывай код. Не хвали. Отвечай по-русски, термины по-английски.

codex
Прочитаю ТЗ и код волны, затем сверю Rust с локальными исходниками Tauri и плагинов. Ничего менять не буду.

use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter, Manager, Runtime};
use tauri_plugin_fs::FsExt;

/// Paths handed to this process on the command line (double-click, "open with").
fn path_args<I: IntoIterator<Item = String>>(argv: I) -> Vec<String> {
    argv.into_iter()
        .skip(1)
        .filter(|arg| !arg.starts_with('-'))
        .collect()
}

/// The dialog plugin widens the fs scope for what the user picked; paths that
/// arrive on the command line have to be granted here or the frontend cannot
/// read them.
fn allow_paths<R: Runtime, M: Manager<R>>(manager: &M, paths: &[String]) {
    if let Some(scope) = manager.try_fs_scope() {
        for path in paths {
            let _ = scope.allow_file(path);
        }
    }
}

/// The frontend asks for these once, at startup.
#[tauri::command]
fn get_launch_args(app: AppHandle) -> Vec<String> {
    let paths = path_args(std::env::args());
    allow_paths(&app, &paths);
    paths
}

pub fn run() {
    tauri::Builder::default()
        // Must be registered first: a second launch hands its argv to the
        // running window instead of opening another one (spec �13).
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_single_instance::init(|app, argv, _cwd| {
            if let Some(window) = app.get_webview_window("main") {
                let _ = window.unminimize();
                let _ = window.show();
                let _ = window.set_focus();
            }
            let paths = path_args(argv);
            if !paths.is_empty() {
                allow_paths(app, &paths);
                let _ = app.emit("open-path", paths);
            }
        }))
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_persisted_scope::init())
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_fs::init())
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_dialog::init())
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_opener::init())
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_window_state::Builder::default().build())
        .invoke_handler(tauri::generate_handler![get_launch_args])
        .run(tauri::generate_context!())
        .expect("error while running Plain");
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::path_args;

    #[test]
    fn drops_the_exe_and_flags() {
        let argv = vec![
            "plain.exe".to_string(),
            "--flag".to_string(),
            r"C:\notes\a.md".to_string(),
        ];
        assert_eq!(path_args(argv), vec![r"C:\notes\a.md".to_string()]);
    }
}

  22   useEffect(
  23     () => watchSystemTheme((resolved) => useStore.getState().syncSystemTheme(resolved)),
  24     [],
  25   );
  26 
  27   // Spec 3: the dirty marker also shows in the window title.
  28   useEffect(() => {
  29     if (!inTauri) return;
  30     const title = doc ? `${doc.title}${doc.dirty ? " " : ""} - Plain` : "Plain";
  31     void getCurrentWindow().setTitle(title);
  32   }, [doc]);
  33 
  34   return (
  35     <div className="app">
  36       <div className="app-body">
  37         {!railCollapsed && <Rail />}
  38         <div className="main">
  39           <ModeBar />
  40           {banner && <Banner banner={banner} />}
  41           {doc ? <DocView doc={doc} /> : <Empty />}
  42           <StatusBar />
  43         </div>
  44       </div>
  45     </div>
  46   );
  47 }

   4 function key(code: string, mods: Partial<KeyLike> = {}): KeyLike {

  29     expect(matchesChord("Ctrl+F", key("KeyF"))).toBe(false);

FILE G:\Claude Projects\md reader\src\app\chords.ts
   1 // Shortcuts are bound by KeyboardEvent.code so they keep working in a
   2 // Russian layout (spec 12). This turns readable chords like "Ctrl+Shift+F"
   3 // into a physical-key description, and into a tinykeys pattern.
   4 
   5 export interface Chord {
   6   ctrl: boolean;
   7   shift: boolean;
   8   alt: boolean;
   9   meta: boolean;
  10   code: string;
  11 }
  12 
  13 export interface KeyLike {
  14   code: string;
  15   ctrlKey: boolean;
  16   shiftKey: boolean;
  17   altKey: boolean;
  18   metaKey: boolean;
  19 }
  20 
  21 const PUNCT: Record<string, string> = {
  22   "/": "Slash",
  23   "\\": "Backslash",
  24   "[": "BracketLeft",
  25   "]": "BracketRight",
  26   ",": "Comma",
  27   ".": "Period",
  28   ";": "Semicolon",
  29   "'": "Quote",
  30   "`": "Backquote",
  31   "-": "Minus",
  32   "=": "Equal",
  33 };
  34 
  35 const NAMED: Record<string, string> = {
  36   space: "Space",
  37   enter: "Enter",
  38   return: "Enter",
  39   esc: "Escape",
  40   escape: "Escape",
  41   tab: "Tab",
  42   backspace: "Backspace",
  43   delete: "Delete",
  44   del: "Delete",
  45   insert: "Insert",
  46   up: "ArrowUp",
  47   down: "ArrowDown",
  48   left: "ArrowLeft",
  49   right: "ArrowRight",
  50   home: "Home",
  51   end: "End",
  52   pageup: "PageUp",
  53   pagedown: "PageDown",
  54 };
  55 
  56 /** Maps one chord token to a KeyboardEvent.code. */
  57 export function toCode(token: string): string {
  58   if (/^[a-z]$/i.test(token)) return "Key" + token.toUpperCase();
  59   if (/^[0-9]$/.test(token)) return "Digit" + token;
  60   if (/^f([1-9]|1[0-9]|2[0-4])$/i.test(token)) return "F" + token.slice(1);
  61   const punct = PUNCT[token];
  62   if (punct) return punct;
  63   const named = NAMED[token.toLowerCase()];
  64   if (named) return named;
  65   // Already a code ("Backquote", "NumpadAdd", ...).
  66   if (/^[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*$/.test(token)) return token;
  67   throw new Error(`unknown key token: ${token}`);
  68 }
  69 
  70 export function parseChord(chord: string): Chord {
  71   const parts = chord.split("+").map((p) => p.trim());
  72   const last = parts.pop();
  73   if (!last) throw new Error(`empty chord: ${chord}`);
  74 
  75   const out: Chord = {
  76     ctrl: false,
  77     shift: false,
  78     alt: false,
  79     meta: false,
  80     code: toCode(last),
  81   };
  82   for (const part of parts) {
  83     switch (part.toLowerCase()) {
  84       case "ctrl":
  85       case "control":
  86         out.ctrl = true;
  87         break;
  88       case "shift":
  89         out.shift = true;
  90         break;
  91       case "alt":
  92       case "option":
  93         out.alt = true;
  94         break;
  95       case "meta":
  96       case "cmd":
  97       case "win":
  98         out.meta = true;
  99         break;
 100       default:
 101         throw new Error(`unknown modifier: ${part}`);
 102     }
 103   }
 104   return out;
 105 }
 106 
 107 /** Exact match, including "no other modifier is held". */
 108 export function matchesChord(chord: string | Chord, event: KeyLike): boolean {
 109   const c = typeof chord === "string" ? parseChord(chord) : chord;
 110   return (
 111     event.code === c.code &&
 112     event.ctrlKey === c.ctrl &&
 113     event.shiftKey === c.shift &&
 114     event.altKey === c.alt &&
 115     event.metaKey === c.meta
 116   );
 117 }
 118 
 119 /**
 120  * tinykeys pattern for the same chord. tinykeys compares the last token
 121  * against event.code as-is, so passing "KeyF" binds the physical key.
 122  */
 123 export function chordToPattern(chord: string): string {
 124   const c = parseChord(chord);
 125   const mods: string[] = [];
 126   if (c.ctrl) mods.push("Control");
 127   if (c.alt) mods.push("Alt");
 128   if (c.shift) mods.push("Shift");
 129   if (c.meta) mods.push("Meta");
 130   return [...mods, c.code].join("+");
 131 }

  11 function reason(error: unknown): string {
  12   return error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  13 }

  15 /** Reads each path into the open list; the first one becomes active. */
  16 export async function openPaths(paths: string[]): Promise<void> {
  17   const store = useStore.getState();
  18   for (const path of paths) {
  19     try {
  20       const text = await readTextFile(path);
  21       store.openDoc(path, text);
  22     } catch (error) {

  24         id: "open-failed",

  26         actions: [{ label: "dismiss", run: () => useStore.getState().dismissBanner("open-failed") }],
  27       });
  28     }
  29   }
  30 }

  32 export async function openFile(): Promise<void> {

  35     .getState()
  36     .settings.library.extensions.map((e) => e.replace(/^\./, ""));
  37   const picked = await open({
  38     multiple: false,
  39     directory: false,
  40     filters: [{ name: "markdown", extensions }],
  41   });
  42   if (typeof picked === "string") await openPaths([picked]);
  43 }
  44 
  45 export async function openLibrary(): Promise<void> {

  48   if (typeof picked !== "string") return;
  49   const store = useStore.getState();
  50   store.setLibraryPath(picked);
  51   store.setRailView("files");
  52   store.setMessage(`library � ${basename(picked)}`);
  53 }
  54 
  55 export function closeActive(): void {
  56   const { activeId, closeDoc } = useStore.getState();
  57   if (activeId) closeDoc(activeId);
  58 }
  59 
  60 export async function toggleFullscreen(): Promise<void> {
  61   if (!inTauri) return;
  62   const win = getCurrentWindow();
  63   await win.setFullscreen(!(await win.isFullscreen()));
  64 }
  65 
  66 export async function toggleAlwaysOnTop(): Promise<void> {
  67   const store = useStore.getState();
  68   const next = !store.alwaysOnTop;
  69   if (inTauri) await getCurrentWindow().setAlwaysOnTop(next);
  70   store.setAlwaysOnTop(next);
  71   store.setMessage(next ? "always on top" : "always on top off");
  72 }

FILE G:\Claude Projects\md reader\src\app\keys.ts
   1 // One keyboard layer for the whole app. Chords are written the way they are
   2 // spelled in the spec and bound by physical key (see chords.ts).
   3 
   4 import { tinykeys } from "tinykeys";
   5 import { chordToPattern, matchesChord } from "./chords";
   6 import {
   7   closeActive,
   8   openFile,
   9   openLibrary,
  10   toggleAlwaysOnTop,
  11   toggleFullscreen,
  12 } from "./commands";
  13 import { useStore } from "./store";
  14 
  15 /**
  16  * WebView2 keeps its own accelerators (spec 12 leaves them on for zoom), but
  17  * these four must not reach it - they are ours, or they would reload the app.
  18  */
  19 const SWALLOW = ["Ctrl+F", "Ctrl+P", "Ctrl+G", "F5"];
  20 
  21 /** chord -> action. New shortcuts go here and nowhere else. */
  22 function bindings(): Record<string, () => void> {
  23   const store = () => useStore.getState();
  24   return {
  25     "Ctrl+/": () => store().toggleMode(),
  26     "Ctrl+Alt+1": () => store().setMode("read"),
  27     "Ctrl+Alt+2": () => store().setMode("edit"),
  28     "Ctrl+\\": () => store().toggleRail(),
  29     "Ctrl+Shift+D": () => store().toggleTheme(),
  30     F11: () => void toggleFullscreen(),
  31     "Ctrl+Shift+A": () => void toggleAlwaysOnTop(),
  32     "Ctrl+O": () => void openFile(),
  33     "Ctrl+Alt+O": () => void openLibrary(),
  34     "Ctrl+W": () => closeActive(),
  35     "Ctrl+Tab": () => store().cycleDoc(1),
  36     "Ctrl+Shift+Tab": () => store().cycleDoc(-1),
  37   };
  38 }
  39 
  40 export function installKeys(): () => void {
  41   const swallow = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
  42     if (SWALLOW.some((chord) => matchesChord(chord, event))) event.preventDefault();
  43   };
  44   window.addEventListener("keydown", swallow, { capture: true });
  45 
  46   const map: Record<string, (event: KeyboardEvent) => void> = {};
  47   for (const [chord, run] of Object.entries(bindings())) {
  48     map[chordToPattern(chord)] = (event) => {
  49       event.preventDefault();
  50       run();
  51     };
  52   }
  53 
  54   const off = tinykeys(window, map, {
  55     // Shortcuts must keep working while the editor (contenteditable) has
  56     // focus, so only auto-repeat and IME composition are skipped.
  57     ignore: (event) => event.repeat || event.isComposing,
  58   });
  59 
  60   return () => {
  61     window.removeEventListener("keydown", swallow, { capture: true });
  62     off();
  63   };
  64 }
FILE G:\Claude Projects\md reader\src\app\paths.ts
   1 // Windows paths, but separators are normalised both ways: dialogs return
   2 // backslashes, hand-written config may not.
   3 
   4 import type { Doc } from "./store";
   5 
   6 export function basename(path: string): string {
   7   const parts = path.split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean);
   8   return parts[parts.length - 1] ?? path;
   9 }
  10 
  11 export function dirname(path: string): string {
  12   const index = Math.max(path.lastIndexOf("\\"), path.lastIndexOf("/"));
  13   return index > 0 ? path.slice(0, index) : "";
  14 }
  15 
  16 function isUnder(path: string, root: string): boolean {
  17   const a = path.replace(/\\/g, "/").toLowerCase();
  18   const b = root.replace(/\\/g, "/").replace(/\/$/, "").toLowerCase();
  19   return a.startsWith(b + "/");
  20 }
  21 
  22 /** `folder / file.md` - first crumb is the root and is drawn bolder. */
  23 export function breadcrumbs(libraryPath: string | null, doc: Doc | null): string[] {
  24   if (!doc) return libraryPath ? [basename(libraryPath)] : [];
  25   if (!doc.path) return [doc.title];
  26   if (libraryPath && isUnder(doc.path, libraryPath)) {
  27     const rel = doc.path.slice(libraryPath.length).replace(/^[\\/]+/, "");
  28     return [basename(libraryPath), ...rel.split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean)];
  29   }
  30   const parts = doc.path.split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean);
  31   return parts.slice(-2);
  32 }

FILE G:\Claude Projects\md reader\src\app\settings.ts
   1 // %APPDATA%\Plain\settings.json - read once at startup (spec 10).
   2 // Broken JSON falls back to defaults and raises a banner; a single bad value
   3 // falls back to that key's default and the rest of the file still applies.
   4 
   5 import { BaseDirectory, exists, readTextFile } from "@tauri-apps/plugin-fs";
   6 import { inTauri } from "./env";
   7 
   8 export type Theme = "system" | "light" | "dark";
   9 export type IndentUnit = "tab" | 2 | 4;
  10 export type Eol = "crlf" | "lf";
  11 
  12 export interface Settings {
  13   appearance: { theme: Theme; fontSize: number; contentWidth: number };
  14   read: { codeWrap: boolean };
  15   edit: { lineNumbers: boolean; indentUnit: IndentUnit };
  16   files: { newFileEol: Eol };
  17   library: { extensions: string[] };
  18 }
  19 
  20 export const SETTINGS_FILE = "settings.json";
  21 
  22 export const DEFAULTS: Settings = {
  23   appearance: { theme: "system", fontSize: 13.5, contentWidth: 560 },
  24   read: { codeWrap: false },
  25   edit: { lineNumbers: true, indentUnit: 2 },
  26   files: { newFileEol: "crlf" },
  27   library: { extensions: [".md", ".markdown"] },
  28 };
  29 
  30 type Json = Record<string, unknown>;
  31 
  32 function section(raw: unknown, key: string): Json {
  33   const root = raw as Json | null;
  34   const value = root && typeof root === "object" ? root[key] : undefined;
  35   return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? (value as Json) : {};
  36 }
  37 
  38 function num(value: unknown, min: number, max: number, fallback: number): number {
  39   return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)
  40     ? Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value))
  41     : fallback;
  42 }
  43 
  44 function bool(value: unknown, fallback: boolean): boolean {
  45   return typeof value === "boolean" ? value : fallback;
  46 }
  47 
  48 function oneOf<T extends string | number>(value: unknown, allowed: readonly T[], fallback: T): T {
  49   return allowed.includes(value as T) ? (value as T) : fallback;
  50 }
  51 
  52 /** Shapes an already-parsed value into Settings, clamping what is out of range. */
  53 export function normalizeSettings(raw: unknown): Settings {
  54   const appearance = section(raw, "appearance");
  55   const read = section(raw, "read");
  56   const edit = section(raw, "edit");
  57   const files = section(raw, "files");
  58   const library = section(raw, "library");
  59 
  60   const extensions = library["extensions"];
  61   const validExtensions =
  62     Array.isArray(extensions) && extensions.every((e) => typeof e === "string" && e.length > 0)
  63       ? (extensions as string[])
  64       : DEFAULTS.library.extensions;
  65 
  66   return {
  67     appearance: {
  68       theme: oneOf(appearance["theme"], ["system", "light", "dark"] as const, DEFAULTS.appearance.theme),
  69       fontSize: num(appearance["fontSize"], 11, 20, DEFAULTS.appearance.fontSize),
  70       contentWidth: num(appearance["contentWidth"], 440, 900, DEFAULTS.appearance.contentWidth),
  71     },
  72     read: { codeWrap: bool(read["codeWrap"], DEFAULTS.read.codeWrap) },
  73     edit: {
  74       lineNumbers: bool(edit["lineNumbers"], DEFAULTS.edit.lineNumbers),
  75       indentUnit: oneOf(edit["indentUnit"], ["tab", 2, 4] as const, DEFAULTS.edit.indentUnit),
  76     },
  77     files: { newFileEol: oneOf(files["newFileEol"], ["crlf", "lf"] as const, DEFAULTS.files.newFileEol) },
  78     library: { extensions: validExtensions },
  79   };
  80 }
  81 
  82 export interface ParsedSettings {
  83   settings: Settings;
  84   invalid: boolean;
  85 }
  86 
  87 /** Parses the file text. Unparseable JSON -> defaults + invalid flag. */
  88 export function parseSettings(text: string): ParsedSettings {
  89   if (text.trim() === "") return { settings: DEFAULTS, invalid: false };
  90   let raw: unknown;
  91   try {
  92     raw = JSON.parse(text);
  93   } catch {
  94     return { settings: DEFAULTS, invalid: true };
  95   }
  96   if (raw === null || typeof raw !== "object" || Array.isArray(raw)) {
  97     return { settings: DEFAULTS, invalid: true };
  98   }
  99   return { settings: normalizeSettings(raw), invalid: false };
 100 }
 101 
 102 /** Reads settings.json from the app data dir. Missing file is not an error. */
 103 export async function loadSettings(): Promise<ParsedSettings> {
 104   if (!inTauri) return { settings: DEFAULTS, invalid: false };
 105   try {
 106     const there = await exists(SETTINGS_FILE, { baseDir: BaseDirectory.AppData });
 107     if (!there) return { settings: DEFAULTS, invalid: false };
 108     const text = await readTextFile(SETTINGS_FILE, { baseDir: BaseDirectory.AppData });
 109     return parseSettings(text);
 110   } catch {
 111     return { settings: DEFAULTS, invalid: true };
 112   }
 113 }

   1 // The whole app state. `text` is already on the document so waves 2 and 3

  50   setTheme: (theme: Theme) => void;
  51   toggleTheme: () => void;
  52   syncSystemTheme: (resolved: "light" | "dark") => void;
  53   toggleRail: () => void;
  54   setRailView: (view: RailView) => void;
  55   setLibraryPath: (path: string | null) => void;
  56   openDoc: (path: string, text: string) => void;
  57   activate: (id: string) => void;
  58   closeDoc: (id: string) => void;
  59   cycleDoc: (step: number) => void;
  60   setMode: (mode: Mode) => void;
  61   toggleMode: () => void;
  62   setMessage: (message: string | null) => void;
  63   showBanner: (banner: Banner) => void;
  64   dismissBanner: (id?: string) => void;
  65   setAlwaysOnTop: (on: boolean) => void;
  66 }
  67 
  68 let messageTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  69 
  70 export const useStore = create<AppState>()((set, get) => ({
  71   docs: [],
  72   activeId: null,
  73   settings: DEFAULTS,
  74   theme: DEFAULTS.appearance.theme,
  75   resolvedTheme: resolveTheme(DEFAULTS.appearance.theme),
  76   railCollapsed: false,
  77   railView: "files",
  78   libraryPath: null,
  79   recent: [],
  80   message: null,
  81   banner: null,
  82   alwaysOnTop: false,
  83 
  84   applySettings: (settings) => {
  85     applyTheme(settings.appearance.theme);
  86     set({
  87       settings,
  88       theme: settings.appearance.theme,
  89       resolvedTheme: resolveTheme(settings.appearance.theme),
  90     });
  91   },
  92 
  93   setTheme: (theme) => {
  94     applyTheme(theme);
  95     set({ theme, resolvedTheme: resolveTheme(theme) });
  96   },
  97 
  98   toggleTheme: () => {
  99     const next = get().resolvedTheme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark";
 100     applyTheme(next);
 101     set({ theme: next, resolvedTheme: next });
 102   },
 103 
 104   syncSystemTheme: (resolved) => {
 105     if (get().theme === "system") set({ resolvedTheme: resolved });
 106   },
 107 
 108   toggleRail: () => set((s) => ({ railCollapsed: !s.railCollapsed })),
 109   setRailView: (railView) => set({ railView }),
 110   setLibraryPath: (libraryPath) => set({ libraryPath }),
 111 
 112   openDoc: (path, text) => {
 113     const existing = get().docs.find((d) => d.path === path);
 114     if (existing) {
 115       set({ activeId: existing.id });
 116       return;
 117     }
 118     const doc: Doc = {
 119       id: path,
 120       path,
 121       title: basename(path),
 122       text,
 123       dirty: false,
 124       mode: "read",
 125       readOnly: false,
 126     };
 127     set((s) => ({
 128       docs: [...s.docs, doc],
 129       activeId: doc.id,
 130       recent: [path, ...s.recent.filter((p) => p !== path)].slice(0, 20),
 131     }));
 132   },
 133 
 134   activate: (id) => set({ activeId: id }),
 135 
 136   closeDoc: (id) =>
 137     set((s) => {
 138       const index = s.docs.findIndex((d) => d.id === id);
 139       if (index < 0) return {};
 140       const docs = s.docs.filter((d) => d.id !== id);
 141       if (s.activeId !== id) return { docs };
 142       const next = docs[Math.min(index, docs.length - 1)];
 143       return { docs, activeId: next ? next.id : null };
 144     }),
 145 
 146   cycleDoc: (step) => {
 147     const { docs, activeId } = get();
 148     if (docs.length < 2) return;
 149     const index = docs.findIndex((d) => d.id === activeId);
 150     const next = docs[(index + step + docs.length) % docs.length];
 151     if (next) set({ activeId: next.id });
 152   },
 153 
 154   setMode: (mode) =>
 155     set((s) => ({
 156       docs: s.docs.map((d) => (d.id === s.activeId ? { ...d, mode } : d)),
 157     })),
 158 
 159   toggleMode: () =>
 160     set((s) => ({
 161       docs: s.docs.map((d) =>
 162         d.id === s.activeId ? { ...d, mode: d.mode === "read" ? "edit" : "read" } : d,
 163       ),
 164     })),
 165 
 166   setMessage: (message) => {
 167     clearTimeout(messageTimer);
 168     set({ message });
 169     if (message !== null) {
 170       messageTimer = setTimeout(() => set({ message: null }), 3000);
 171     }
 172   },
 173 
 174   showBanner: (banner) => set({ banner }),
 175   dismissBanner: (id) => set((s) => (id && s.banner?.id !== id ? {} : { banner: null })),
 176   setAlwaysOnTop: (alwaysOnTop) => set({ alwaysOnTop }),
 177 }));
 178 
 179 export function activeDoc(state: AppState): Doc | null {
 180   return state.docs.find((d) => d.id === state.activeId) ?? null;
 181 }
FILE G:\Claude Projects\md reader\src\app\theme.ts
   1 // "system" leaves data-theme off so the CSS media query decides; light/dark
   2 // stamp the attribute. matchMedia only tells us which label to show.
   3 
   4 import type { Theme } from "./settings";
   5 
   6 const dark = typeof window !== "undefined" ? window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)") : null;
   7 
   8 export function systemTheme(): "light" | "dark" {
   9   return dark?.matches ? "dark" : "light";
  10 }
  11 
  12 export function resolveTheme(theme: Theme): "light" | "dark" {
  13   return theme === "system" ? systemTheme() : theme;
  14 }
  15 
  16 export function applyTheme(theme: Theme): void {
  17   const root = document.documentElement;
  18   if (theme === "system") root.removeAttribute("data-theme");
  19   else root.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);
  20 }
  21 
  22 /** Calls back when the OS theme changes; only matters while theme is "system". */
  23 export function watchSystemTheme(onChange: (resolved: "light" | "dark") => void): () => void {
  24   if (!dark) return () => {};
  25   const handler = () => onChange(dark.matches ? "dark" : "light");
  26   dark.addEventListener("change", handler);
  27   return () => dark.removeEventListener("change", handler);
  28 }

FILE G:\Claude Projects\md reader\src\ui\ModeBar.tsx
   1 import { Fragment } from "react";
   2 import { breadcrumbs } from "../app/paths";
   3 import { activeDoc, useStore, type Mode } from "../app/store";
   4 
   5 const MODES: Mode[] = ["read", "edit"];
   6 
   7 /** Root is bold, the file name is plain fg, anything between is muted. */
   8 function crumbClass(index: number, total: number): string {
   9   if (index === 0) return "crumb crumb-root";
  10   return index === total - 1 ? "crumb crumb-leaf" : "crumb";
  11 }
  12 
  13 export function ModeBar() {
  14   const railCollapsed = useStore((s) => s.railCollapsed);
  15   const toggleRail = useStore((s) => s.toggleRail);
  16   const libraryPath = useStore((s) => s.libraryPath);
  17   const doc = useStore(activeDoc);
  18   const setMode = useStore((s) => s.setMode);
  19 
  20   const crumbs = breadcrumbs(libraryPath, doc);
  21 
  22   return (
  23     <div className="modebar">
  24       <div className="modebar-left">
  25         {railCollapsed && (
  26           <button className="rail-peek" onClick={toggleRail}>
  27             ? files
  28           </button>
  29         )}
  30         {crumbs.length > 0 && (
  31           <div className="crumbs">
  32             {crumbs.map((crumb, i) => (
  33               <Fragment key={i}>
  34                 {i > 0 && <span className="crumb-sep">/</span>}
  35                 <span className={crumbClass(i, crumbs.length)}>{crumb}</span>
  36               </Fragment>
  37             ))}
  38             {doc?.dirty && <span className="dot"></span>}
  39           </div>
  40         )}
  41       </div>
  42 
  43       <div className="modes">
  44         {MODES.map((mode) => (
  45           <button
  46             key={mode}
  47             className={"mode" + (doc?.mode === mode ? " is-active" : "")}
  48             onClick={() => doc && setMode(mode)}
  49           >
  50             {mode}
  51           </button>
  52         ))}
  53       </div>
  54     </div>
  55   );
  56 }

FILE G:\Claude Projects\md reader\src\ui\Rail.tsx
   1 import { openLibrary } from "../app/commands";
   2 import { basename } from "../app/paths";
   3 import { useStore } from "../app/store";
   4 import "./rail.css";
   5 
   6 export function Rail() {
   7   const docs = useStore((s) => s.docs);
   8   const activeId = useStore((s) => s.activeId);
   9   const railView = useStore((s) => s.railView);
  10   const libraryPath = useStore((s) => s.libraryPath);
  11   const activate = useStore((s) => s.activate);
  12   const closeDoc = useStore((s) => s.closeDoc);
  13   const setRailView = useStore((s) => s.setRailView);
  14 
  15   return (
  16     <aside className="rail">
  17       <div className="rail-search">
  18         <span>? search</span>
  19         <span className="rail-hint">ctrl k</span>
  20       </div>
  21 
  22       <section className="rail-section">
  23         <div className="section-title rail-heading">open</div>
  24         {docs.length === 0 ? (
  25           <div className="rail-empty">nothing open</div>
  26         ) : (
  27           docs.map((doc) => (
  28             <button
  29               key={doc.id}
  30               className={"rail-item" + (doc.id === activeId ? " is-active" : "")}
  31               onClick={() => activate(doc.id)}
  32               onAuxClick={(e) => {
  33                 if (e.button === 1) closeDoc(doc.id);
  34               }}
  35             >
  36               <span className="rail-item-name">{doc.title}</span>
  37               {doc.dirty && <span className="dot">?</span>}
  38             </button>
  39           ))
  40         )}
  41       </section>
  42 
  43       <section className="rail-section">
  44         <div className="section-title rail-heading">{railView}</div>
  45         {railView === "files" ? (
  46           libraryPath ? (
  47             <div className="rail-empty">{basename(libraryPath)}</div>
  48           ) : (
  49             <button className="rail-empty link" onClick={() => void openLibrary()}>
  50               open a library
  51             </button>
  52           )
  53         ) : (
  54           <div className="rail-empty">no headings</div>
  55         )}

   1 import { activeDoc, useStore } from "../app/store";
   2 
   3 const segmenter =
   4   typeof Intl !== "undefined" && "Segmenter" in Intl
   5     ? new Intl.Segmenter(undefined, { granularity: "word" })
   6     : null;
   7 
   8 /** Fenced-code exclusion belongs to the read pipeline (wave 2). */
   9 export function countWords(text: string): number {
  10   if (!segmenter) return text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
  11   let count = 0;
  12   for (const part of segmenter.segment(text)) if (part.isWordLike) count += 1;
  13   return count;
  14 }
  15 
  16 export function StatusBar() {
  17   const message = useStore((s) => s.message);
  18   const doc = useStore(activeDoc);
  19   const resolvedTheme = useStore((s) => s.resolvedTheme);
  20   const toggleTheme = useStore((s) => s.toggleTheme);
  21 
  22   const state = doc ? (doc.readOnly ? "read-only" : doc.dirty ? "unsaved" : "saved") : null;
  23 
  24   return (
  25     <div className="statusbar">
  26       {/* Left slot also carries `ln 9, col 118` once the editor exists. */}
  27       <span className="status-left">{message}</span>
  28       <span className="status-right">
  29         {doc && <span>utf-8</span>}
  30         {doc && <span>{countWords(doc.text).toLocaleString("en-US")} words</span>}
  31         {state && <span>{state}</span>}
  32         <button className="link theme-toggle" onClick={toggleTheme}>
  33           {resolvedTheme === "dark" ? "? dark" : "? light"}
  34         </button>
  35       </span>
  36     </div>
  37   );
  38 }

  16   const railCollapsed = useStore((s) => s.railCollapsed);
  17   const banner = useStore((s) => s.banner);
  18   const doc = useStore(activeDoc);
  19 
  20   useEffect(() => installKeys(), []);
  21 
  22   useEffect(
  23     () => watchSystemTheme((resolved) => useStore.getState().syncSystemTheme(resolved)),
  24     [],
  25   );
  26 
  27   // Spec 3: the dirty marker also shows in the window title.
  28   useEffect(() => {
  29     if (!inTauri) return;
  30     const title = doc ? `${doc.title}${doc.dirty ? " " : ""} - Plain` : "Plain";
  31     void getCurrentWindow().setTitle(title);
  32   }, [doc]);
  33 
  34   return (
  35     <div className="app">
  36       <div className="app-body">
  37         {!railCollapsed && <Rail />}
  38         <div className="main">
  39           <ModeBar />
  40           {banner && <Banner banner={banner} />}
  41           {doc ? <DocView doc={doc} /> : <Empty />}
  42           <StatusBar />
  43         </div>
  44       </div>
  45     </div>
  46   );
  47 }
FILE bootstrap.ts
   1 // Startup: settings, then the paths this launch was asked to open.
   2 
   3 import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
   4 import { listen } from "@tauri-apps/api/event";
   5 import { openPaths } from "./commands";
   6 import { inTauri } from "./env";
   7 import { loadSettings } from "./settings";
   8 import { useStore } from "./store";
   9 
  10 export async function bootstrap(): Promise<void> {
  11   const { settings, invalid } = await loadSettings();
  12   const store = useStore.getState();
  13   store.applySettings(settings);
  14   if (invalid) {
  15     store.showBanner({
  16       id: "settings",
  17       text: "settings.json is invalid",
  18       actions: [{ label: "dismiss", run: () => useStore.getState().dismissBanner("settings") }],
  19     });
  20   }
  21 
  22   if (!inTauri) return;
  23 
  24   // A second launch hands its argv over to this window (single instance).
  25   await listen<string[]>("open-path", (event) => void openPaths(event.payload));
  26 
  27   const args = await invoke<string[]>("get_launch_args");
  28   if (args.length > 0) await openPaths(args);
  29 }
FILE chords.ts
   1 // Shortcuts are bound by KeyboardEvent.code so they keep working in a
   2 // Russian layout (spec 12). This turns readable chords like "Ctrl+Shift+F"
   3 // into a physical-key description, and into a tinykeys pattern.
   4 
   5 export interface Chord {
   6   ctrl: boolean;
   7   shift: boolean;
   8   alt: boolean;
   9   meta: boolean;
  10   code: string;
  11 }
  12 
  13 export interface KeyLike {
  14   code: string;
  15   ctrlKey: boolean;
  16   shiftKey: boolean;
  17   altKey: boolean;
  18   metaKey: boolean;
  19 }
  20 
  21 const PUNCT: Record<string, string> = {
  22   "/": "Slash",
  23   "\\": "Backslash",
  24   "[": "BracketLeft",
  25   "]": "BracketRight",
  26   ",": "Comma",
  27   ".": "Period",
  28   ";": "Semicolon",
  29   "'": "Quote",
  30   "`": "Backquote",
  31   "-": "Minus",
  32   "=": "Equal",
  33 };
  34 
  35 const NAMED: Record<string, string> = {
  36   space: "Space",
  37   enter: "Enter",
  38   return: "Enter",
  39   esc: "Escape",
  40   escape: "Escape",
  41   tab: "Tab",
  42   backspace: "Backspace",
  43   delete: "Delete",
  44   del: "Delete",
  45   insert: "Insert",
  46   up: "ArrowUp",
  47   down: "ArrowDown",
  48   left: "ArrowLeft",
  49   right: "ArrowRight",
  50   home: "Home",
  51   end: "End",
  52   pageup: "PageUp",
  53   pagedown: "PageDown",
  54 };
  55 
  56 /** Maps one chord token to a KeyboardEvent.code. */
  57 export function toCode(token: string): string {
  58   if (/^[a-z]$/i.test(token)) return "Key" + token.toUpperCase();
  59   if (/^[0-9]$/.test(token)) return "Digit" + token;
  60   if (/^f([1-9]|1[0-9]|2[0-4])$/i.test(token)) return "F" + token.slice(1);
  61   const punct = PUNCT[token];
  62   if (punct) return punct;
  63   const named = NAMED[token.toLowerCase()];
  64   if (named) return named;
  65   // Already a code ("Backquote", "NumpadAdd", ...).
  66   if (/^[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*$/.test(token)) return token;
  67   throw new Error(`unknown key token: ${token}`);
  68 }
  69 
  70 export function parseChord(chord: string): Chord {
  71   const parts = chord.split("+").map((p) => p.trim());
  72   const last = parts.pop();
  73   if (!last) throw new Error(`empty chord: ${chord}`);
  74 
  75   const out: Chord = {
  76     ctrl: false,
  77     shift: false,
  78     alt: false,
  79     meta: false,
  80     code: toCode(last),
  81   };
  82   for (const part of parts) {
  83     switch (part.toLowerCase()) {
  84       case "ctrl":
  85       case "control":
  86         out.ctrl = true;
  87         break;
  88       case "shift":
  89         out.shift = true;
  90         break;
  91       case "alt":
  92       case "option":
  93         out.alt = true;
  94         break;
  95       case "meta":
  96       case "cmd":
  97       case "win":
  98         out.meta = true;
  99         break;
 100       default:
 101         throw new Error(`unknown modifier: ${part}`);
 102     }
 103   }
 104   return out;
 105 }
 106 
 107 /** Exact match, including "no other modifier is held". */
 108 export function matchesChord(chord: string | Chord, event: KeyLike): boolean {
 109   const c = typeof chord === "string" ? parseChord(chord) : chord;
 110   return (
 111     event.code === c.code &&
 112     event.ctrlKey === c.ctrl &&
 113     event.shiftKey === c.shift &&
 114     event.altKey === c.alt &&
 115     event.metaKey === c.meta
 116   );
 117 }
 118 
 119 /**
 120  * tinykeys pattern for the same chord. tinykeys compares the last token
 121  * against event.code as-is, so passing "KeyF" binds the physical key.
 122  */
 123 export function chordToPattern(chord: string): string {
 124   const c = parseChord(chord);
 125   const mods: string[] = [];
 126   if (c.ctrl) mods.push("Control");
 127   if (c.alt) mods.push("Alt");
 128   if (c.shift) mods.push("Shift");
 129   if (c.meta) mods.push("Meta");
 130   return [...mods, c.code].join("+");
 131 }
FILE commands.ts
   1 // Everything a shortcut, a menu or a link can trigger. Wave 1 covers the
   2 // window and opening files; saving arrives in wave 4.
   3 
   4 import { open } from "@tauri-apps/plugin-dialog";
   5 import { readTextFile } from "@tauri-apps/plugin-fs";
   6 import { getCurrentWindow } from "@tauri-apps/api/window";
   7 import { inTauri } from "./env";
   8 import { basename } from "./paths";
   9 import { useStore } from "./store";
  10 
  11 function reason(error: unknown): string {
  12   return error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  13 }
  14 
  15 /** Reads each path into the open list; the first one becomes active. */
  16 export async function openPaths(paths: string[]): Promise<void> {
  17   const store = useStore.getState();
  18   for (const path of paths) {
  19     try {
  20       const text = await readTextFile(path);
  21       store.openDoc(path, text);
  22     } catch (error) {
  23       store.showBanner({
  24         id: "open-failed",
  25         text: `couldn't open ${basename(path)} - ${reason(error)}`,
  26         actions: [{ label: "dismiss", run: () => useStore.getState().dismissBanner("open-failed") }],
  27       });
  28     }
  29   }
  30 }
  31 
  32 export async function openFile(): Promise<void> {
  33   if (!inTauri) return;
  34   const extensions = useStore
  35     .getState()
  36     .settings.library.extensions.map((e) => e.replace(/^\./, ""));
  37   const picked = await open({
  38     multiple: false,
  39     directory: false,
  40     filters: [{ name: "markdown", extensions }],
  41   });
  42   if (typeof picked === "string") await openPaths([picked]);
  43 }
  44 
  45 export async function openLibrary(): Promise<void> {
  46   if (!inTauri) return;
  47   const picked = await open({ directory: true, multiple: false });
  48   if (typeof picked !== "string") return;
  49   const store = useStore.getState();
  50   store.setLibraryPath(picked);
  51   store.setRailView("files");
  52   store.setMessage(`library � ${basename(picked)}`);
  53 }
  54 
  55 export function closeActive(): void {
  56   const { activeId, closeDoc } = useStore.getState();
  57   if (activeId) closeDoc(activeId);
  58 }
  59 
  60 export async function toggleFullscreen(): Promise<void> {
  61   if (!inTauri) return;
  62   const win = getCurrentWindow();
  63   await win.setFullscreen(!(await win.isFullscreen()));
  64 }
  65 
  66 export async function toggleAlwaysOnTop(): Promise<void> {
  67   const store = useStore.getState();
  68   const next = !store.alwaysOnTop;
  69   if (inTauri) await getCurrentWindow().setAlwaysOnTop(next);
  70   store.setAlwaysOnTop(next);
  71   store.setMessage(next ? "always on top" : "always on top off");
  72 }
FILE env.ts
   1 // The UI also has to render in a plain browser during `vite dev`, so every
   2 // Tauri call goes behind this flag.
   3 export const inTauri =
   4   typeof window !== "undefined" && "__TAURI_INTERNALS__" in window;
FILE keys.ts
   1 // One keyboard layer for the whole app. Chords are written the way they are
   2 // spelled in the spec and bound by physical key (see chords.ts).
   3 
   4 import { tinykeys } from "tinykeys";
   5 import { chordToPattern, matchesChord } from "./chords";
   6 import {
   7   closeActive,
   8   openFile,
   9   openLibrary,
  10   toggleAlwaysOnTop,
  11   toggleFullscreen,
  12 } from "./commands";
  13 import { useStore } from "./store";
  14 
  15 /**
  16  * WebView2 keeps its own accelerators (spec 12 leaves them on for zoom), but
  17  * these four must not reach it - they are ours, or they would reload the app.
  18  */
  19 const SWALLOW = ["Ctrl+F", "Ctrl+P", "Ctrl+G", "F5"];
  20 
  21 /** chord -> action. New shortcuts go here and nowhere else. */
  22 function bindings(): Record<string, () => void> {
  23   const store = () => useStore.getState();
  24   return {
  25     "Ctrl+/": () => store().toggleMode(),
  26     "Ctrl+Alt+1": () => store().setMode("read"),
  27     "Ctrl+Alt+2": () => store().setMode("edit"),
  28     "Ctrl+\\": () => store().toggleRail(),
  29     "Ctrl+Shift+D": () => store().toggleTheme(),
  30     F11: () => void toggleFullscreen(),
  31     "Ctrl+Shift+A": () => void toggleAlwaysOnTop(),
  32     "Ctrl+O": () => void openFile(),
  33     "Ctrl+Alt+O": () => void openLibrary(),
  34     "Ctrl+W": () => closeActive(),
  35     "Ctrl+Tab": () => store().cycleDoc(1),
  36     "Ctrl+Shift+Tab": () => store().cycleDoc(-1),
  37   };
  38 }
  39 
  40 export function installKeys(): () => void {
  41   const swallow = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
  42     if (SWALLOW.some((chord) => matchesChord(chord, event))) event.preventDefault();
  43   };
  44   window.addEventListener("keydown", swallow, { capture: true });
  45 
  46   const map: Record<string, (event: KeyboardEvent) => void> = {};
  47   for (const [chord, run] of Object.entries(bindings())) {
  48     map[chordToPattern(chord)] = (event) => {
  49       event.preventDefault();
  50       run();
  51     };
  52   }
  53 
  54   const off = tinykeys(window, map, {
  55     // Shortcuts must keep working while the editor (contenteditable) has
  56     // focus, so only auto-repeat and IME composition are skipped.
  57     ignore: (event) => event.repeat || event.isComposing,
  58   });
  59 
  60   return () => {
  61     window.removeEventListener("keydown", swallow, { capture: true });
  62     off();
  63   };
  64 }
FILE paths.ts
   1 // Windows paths, but separators are normalised both ways: dialogs return
   2 // backslashes, hand-written config may not.
   3 
   4 import type { Doc } from "./store";
   5 
   6 export function basename(path: string): string {
   7   const parts = path.split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean);
   8   return parts[parts.length - 1] ?? path;
   9 }
  10 
  11 export function dirname(path: string): string {
  12   const index = Math.max(path.lastIndexOf("\\"), path.lastIndexOf("/"));
  13   return index > 0 ? path.slice(0, index) : "";
  14 }
  15 
  16 function isUnder(path: string, root: string): boolean {
  17   const a = path.replace(/\\/g, "/").toLowerCase();
  18   const b = root.replace(/\\/g, "/").replace(/\/$/, "").toLowerCase();
  19   return a.startsWith(b + "/");
  20 }
  21 
  22 /** `folder / file.md` - first crumb is the root and is drawn bolder. */
  23 export function breadcrumbs(libraryPath: string | null, doc: Doc | null): string[] {
  24   if (!doc) return libraryPath ? [basename(libraryPath)] : [];
  25   if (!doc.path) return [doc.title];
  26   if (libraryPath && isUnder(doc.path, libraryPath)) {
  27     const rel = doc.path.slice(libraryPath.length).replace(/^[\\/]+/, "");
  28     return [basename(libraryPath), ...rel.split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean)];
  29   }
  30   const parts = doc.path.split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean);
  31   return parts.slice(-2);
  32 }
FILE settings.ts
   1 // %APPDATA%\Plain\settings.json - read once at startup (spec 10).
   2 // Broken JSON falls back to defaults and raises a banner; a single bad value
   3 // falls back to that key's default and the rest of the file still applies.
   4 
   5 import { BaseDirectory, exists, readTextFile } from "@tauri-apps/plugin-fs";
   6 import { inTauri } from "./env";
   7 
   8 export type Theme = "system" | "light" | "dark";
   9 export type IndentUnit = "tab" | 2 | 4;
  10 export type Eol = "crlf" | "lf";
  11 
  12 export interface Settings {
  13   appearance: { theme: Theme; fontSize: number; contentWidth: number };
  14   read: { codeWrap: boolean };
  15   edit: { lineNumbers: boolean; indentUnit: IndentUnit };
  16   files: { newFileEol: Eol };
  17   library: { extensions: string[] };
  18 }
  19 
  20 export const SETTINGS_FILE = "settings.json";
  21 
  22 export const DEFAULTS: Settings = {
  23   appearance: { theme: "system", fontSize: 13.5, contentWidth: 560 },
  24   read: { codeWrap: false },
  25   edit: { lineNumbers: true, indentUnit: 2 },
  26   files: { newFileEol: "crlf" },
  27   library: { extensions: [".md", ".markdown"] },
  28 };
  29 
  30 type Json = Record<string, unknown>;
  31 
  32 function section(raw: unknown, key: string): Json {
  33   const root = raw as Json | null;
  34   const value = root && typeof root === "object" ? root[key] : undefined;
  35   return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? (value as Json) : {};
  36 }
  37 
  38 function num(value: unknown, min: number, max: number, fallback: number): number {
  39   return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)
  40     ? Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value))
  41     : fallback;
  42 }
  43 
  44 function bool(value: unknown, fallback: boolean): boolean {
  45   return typeof value === "boolean" ? value : fallback;
  46 }
  47 
  48 function oneOf<T extends string | number>(value: unknown, allowed: readonly T[], fallback: T): T {
  49   return allowed.includes(value as T) ? (value as T) : fallback;
  50 }
  51 
  52 /** Shapes an already-parsed value into Settings, clamping what is out of range. */
  53 export function normalizeSettings(raw: unknown): Settings {
  54   const appearance = section(raw, "appearance");
  55   const read = section(raw, "read");
  56   const edit = section(raw, "edit");
  57   const files = section(raw, "files");
  58   const library = section(raw, "library");
  59 
  60   const extensions = library["extensions"];
  61   const validExtensions =
  62     Array.isArray(extensions) && extensions.every((e) => typeof e === "string" && e.length > 0)
  63       ? (extensions as string[])
  64       : DEFAULTS.library.extensions;
  65 
  66   return {
  67     appearance: {
  68       theme: oneOf(appearance["theme"], ["system", "light", "dark"] as const, DEFAULTS.appearance.theme),
  69       fontSize: num(appearance["fontSize"], 11, 20, DEFAULTS.appearance.fontSize),
  70       contentWidth: num(appearance["contentWidth"], 440, 900, DEFAULTS.appearance.contentWidth),
  71     },
  72     read: { codeWrap: bool(read["codeWrap"], DEFAULTS.read.codeWrap) },
  73     edit: {
  74       lineNumbers: bool(edit["lineNumbers"], DEFAULTS.edit.lineNumbers),
  75       indentUnit: oneOf(edit["indentUnit"], ["tab", 2, 4] as const, DEFAULTS.edit.indentUnit),
  76     },
  77     files: { newFileEol: oneOf(files["newFileEol"], ["crlf", "lf"] as const, DEFAULTS.files.newFileEol) },
  78     library: { extensions: validExtensions },
  79   };
  80 }
  81 
  82 export interface ParsedSettings {
  83   settings: Settings;
  84   invalid: boolean;
  85 }
  86 
  87 /** Parses the file text. Unparseable JSON -> defaults + invalid flag. */
  88 export function parseSettings(text: string): ParsedSettings {
  89   if (text.trim() === "") return { settings: DEFAULTS, invalid: false };
  90   let raw: unknown;
  91   try {
  92     raw = JSON.parse(text);
  93   } catch {
  94     return { settings: DEFAULTS, invalid: true };
  95   }
  96   if (raw === null || typeof raw !== "object" || Array.isArray(raw)) {
  97     return { settings: DEFAULTS, invalid: true };
  98   }
  99   return { settings: normalizeSettings(raw), invalid: false };
 100 }
 101 
 102 /** Reads settings.json from the app data dir. Missing file is not an error. */
 103 export async function loadSettings(): Promise<ParsedSettings> {
 104   if (!inTauri) return { settings: DEFAULTS, invalid: false };
 105   try {
 106     const there = await exists(SETTINGS_FILE, { baseDir: BaseDirectory.AppData });
 107     if (!there) return { settings: DEFAULTS, invalid: false };
 108     const text = await readTextFile(SETTINGS_FILE, { baseDir: BaseDirectory.AppData });
 109     return parseSettings(text);
 110   } catch {
 111     return { settings: DEFAULTS, invalid: true };
 112   }
 113 }
FILE store.ts
   1 // The whole app state. `text` is already on the document so waves 2 and 3
   2 // can fill it in without reshaping anything.
   3 
   4 import { create } from "zustand";
   5 import { DEFAULTS, type Settings, type Theme } from "./settings";
   6 import { applyTheme, resolveTheme } from "./theme";
   7 import { basename } from "./paths";
   8 
   9 export type Mode = "read" | "edit";
  10 export type RailView = "files" | "outline";
  11 
  12 export interface Doc {
  13   id: string;
  14   /** null while the buffer has never been written to disk. */
  15   path: string | null;
  16   title: string;
  17   text: string;
  18   dirty: boolean;
  19   mode: Mode;
  20   readOnly: boolean;
  21 }
  22 
  23 export interface BannerAction {
  24   label: string;
  25   run: () => void;
  26 }
  27 
  28 export interface Banner {
  29   id: string;
  30   text: string;
  31   actions?: BannerAction[];
  32 }
  33 
  34 interface AppState {
  35   docs: Doc[];
  36   activeId: string | null;
  37   settings: Settings;
  38   theme: Theme;
  39   resolvedTheme: "light" | "dark";
  40   railCollapsed: boolean;
  41   railView: RailView;
  42   libraryPath: string | null;
  43   recent: string[];
  44   /** Transient status-bar message; clears itself after 3 s. */
  45   message: string | null;
  46   banner: Banner | null;
  47   alwaysOnTop: boolean;
  48 
  49   applySettings: (settings: Settings) => void;
  50   setTheme: (theme: Theme) => void;
  51   toggleTheme: () => void;
  52   syncSystemTheme: (resolved: "light" | "dark") => void;
  53   toggleRail: () => void;
  54   setRailView: (view: RailView) => void;
  55   setLibraryPath: (path: string | null) => void;
  56   openDoc: (path: string, text: string) => void;
  57   activate: (id: string) => void;
  58   closeDoc: (id: string) => void;
  59   cycleDoc: (step: number) => void;
  60   setMode: (mode: Mode) => void;
  61   toggleMode: () => void;
  62   setMessage: (message: string | null) => void;
  63   showBanner: (banner: Banner) => void;
  64   dismissBanner: (id?: string) => void;
  65   setAlwaysOnTop: (on: boolean) => void;
  66 }
  67 
  68 let messageTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  69 
  70 export const useStore = create<AppState>()((set, get) => ({
  71   docs: [],
  72   activeId: null,
  73   settings: DEFAULTS,
  74   theme: DEFAULTS.appearance.theme,
  75   resolvedTheme: resolveTheme(DEFAULTS.appearance.theme),
  76   railCollapsed: false,
  77   railView: "files",
  78   libraryPath: null,
  79   recent: [],
  80   message: null,
  81   banner: null,
  82   alwaysOnTop: false,
  83 
  84   applySettings: (settings) => {
  85     applyTheme(settings.appearance.theme);
  86     set({
  87       settings,
  88       theme: settings.appearance.theme,
  89       resolvedTheme: resolveTheme(settings.appearance.theme),
  90     });
  91   },
  92 
  93   setTheme: (theme) => {
  94     applyTheme(theme);
  95     set({ theme, resolvedTheme: resolveTheme(theme) });
  96   },
  97 
  98   toggleTheme: () => {
  99     const next = get().resolvedTheme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark";
 100     applyTheme(next);
 101     set({ theme: next, resolvedTheme: next });
 102   },
 103 
 104   syncSystemTheme: (resolved) => {
 105     if (get().theme === "system") set({ resolvedTheme: resolved });
 106   },
 107 
 108   toggleRail: () => set((s) => ({ railCollapsed: !s.railCollapsed })),
 109   setRailView: (railView) => set({ railView }),
 110   setLibraryPath: (libraryPath) => set({ libraryPath }),
 111 
 112   openDoc: (path, text) => {
 113     const existing = get().docs.find((d) => d.path === path);
 114     if (existing) {
 115       set({ activeId: existing.id });
 116       return;
 117     }
 118     const doc: Doc = {
 119       id: path,
 120       path,
 121       title: basename(path),
 122       text,
 123       dirty: false,
 124       mode: "read",
 125       readOnly: false,
 126     };
 127     set((s) => ({
 128       docs: [...s.docs, doc],
 129       activeId: doc.id,
 130       recent: [path, ...s.recent.filter((p) => p !== path)].slice(0, 20),
 131     }));
 132   },
 133 
 134   activate: (id) => set({ activeId: id }),
 135 
 136   closeDoc: (id) =>
 137     set((s) => {
 138       const index = s.docs.findIndex((d) => d.id === id);
 139       if (index < 0) return {};
 140       const docs = s.docs.filter((d) => d.id !== id);
 141       if (s.activeId !== id) return { docs };
 142       const next = docs[Math.min(index, docs.length - 1)];
 143       return { docs, activeId: next ? next.id : null };
 144     }),
 145 
 146   cycleDoc: (step) => {
 147     const { docs, activeId } = get();
 148     if (docs.length < 2) return;
 149     const index = docs.findIndex((d) => d.id === activeId);
 150     const next = docs[(index + step + docs.length) % docs.length];
 151     if (next) set({ activeId: next.id });
 152   },
 153 
 154   setMode: (mode) =>
 155     set((s) => ({
 156       docs: s.docs.map((d) => (d.id === s.activeId ? { ...d, mode } : d)),
 157     })),
 158 
 159   toggleMode: () =>
 160     set((s) => ({
 161       docs: s.docs.map((d) =>
 162         d.id === s.activeId ? { ...d, mode: d.mode === "read" ? "edit" : "read" } : d,
 163       ),
 164     })),
 165 
 166   setMessage: (message) => {
 167     clearTimeout(messageTimer);
 168     set({ message });
 169     if (message !== null) {
 170       messageTimer = setTimeout(() => set({ message: null }), 3000);
 171     }
 172   },
 173 
 174   showBanner: (banner) => set({ banner }),
 175   dismissBanner: (id) => set((s) => (id && s.banner?.id !== id ? {} : { banner: null })),
 176   setAlwaysOnTop: (alwaysOnTop) => set({ alwaysOnTop }),
 177 }));
 178 
 179 export function activeDoc(state: AppState): Doc | null {
 180   return state.docs.find((d) => d.id === state.activeId) ?? null;
 181 }
FILE theme.ts
   1 // "system" leaves data-theme off so the CSS media query decides; light/dark
   2 // stamp the attribute. matchMedia only tells us which label to show.
   3 
   4 import type { Theme } from "./settings";
   5 
   6 const dark = typeof window !== "undefined" ? window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)") : null;
   7 
   8 export function systemTheme(): "light" | "dark" {
   9   return dark?.matches ? "dark" : "light";
  10 }
  11 
  12 export function resolveTheme(theme: Theme): "light" | "dark" {
  13   return theme === "system" ? systemTheme() : theme;
  14 }
  15 
  16 export function applyTheme(theme: Theme): void {
  17   const root = document.documentElement;
  18   if (theme === "system") root.removeAttribute("data-theme");
  19   else root.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);
  20 }
  21 
  22 /** Calls back when the OS theme changes; only matters while theme is "system". */
  23 export function watchSystemTheme(onChange: (resolved: "light" | "dark") => void): () => void {
  24   if (!dark) return () => {};
  25   const handler = () => onChange(dark.matches ? "dark" : "light");
  26   dark.addEventListener("change", handler);
  27   return () => dark.removeEventListener("change", handler);
  28 }

// Shortcuts are bound by KeyboardEvent.code so they keep working in a
// Russian layout (spec �12). This turns readable chords like "Ctrl+Shift+F"
// into a physical-key description, and into a tinykeys pattern.

export interface Chord {
  ctrl: boolean;
  shift: boolean;
  alt: boolean;
  meta: boolean;
  code: string;
}

export interface KeyLike {
  code: string;
  ctrlKey: boolean;
  shiftKey: boolean;
  altKey: boolean;
  metaKey: boolean;
}

const PUNCT: Record<string, string> = {
  "/": "Slash",
  "\\": "Backslash",
  "[": "BracketLeft",
  "]": "BracketRight",
  ",": "Comma",
  ".": "Period",
  ";": "Semicolon",
  "'": "Quote",
  "`": "Backquote",
  "-": "Minus",
  "=": "Equal",
};

const NAMED: Record<string, string> = {
  space: "Space",
  enter: "Enter",
  return: "Enter",
  esc: "Escape",
  escape: "Escape",
  tab: "Tab",
  backspace: "Backspace",
  delete: "Delete",
  del: "Delete",
  insert: "Insert",
  up: "ArrowUp",
  down: "ArrowDown",
  left: "ArrowLeft",
  right: "ArrowRight",
  home: "Home",
  end: "End",
  pageup: "PageUp",
  pagedown: "PageDown",
};

/** Maps one chord token to a KeyboardEvent.code. */
export function toCode(token: string): string {
  if (/^[a-z]$/i.test(token)) return "Key" + token.toUpperCase();
  if (/^[0-9]$/.test(token)) return "Digit" + token;
  if (/^f([1-9]|1[0-9]|2[0-4])$/i.test(token)) return "F" + token.slice(1);
  const punct = PUNCT[token];
  if (punct) return punct;
  const named = NAMED[token.toLowerCase()];
  if (named) return named;
  // Already a code ("Backquote", "NumpadAdd", ...).
  if (/^[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*$/.test(token)) return token;
  throw new Error(`unknown key token: ${token}`);
}

export function parseChord(chord: string): Chord {
  const parts = chord.split("+").map((p) => p.trim());
  const last = parts.pop();
  if (!last) throw new Error(`empty chord: ${chord}`);

  const out: Chord = {
    ctrl: false,
    shift: false,
    alt: false,
    meta: false,
    code: toCode(last),
  };
  for (const part of parts) {
    switch (part.toLowerCase()) {
      case "ctrl":
      case "control":
        out.ctrl = true;
        break;
      case "shift":
        out.shift = true;
        break;
      case "alt":
      case "option":
        out.alt = true;
        break;
      case "meta":
      case "cmd":
      case "win":
        out.meta = true;
        break;
      default:
        throw new Error(`unknown modifier: ${part}`);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Exact match, including "no other modifier is held". */
export function matchesChord(chord: string | Chord, event: KeyLike): boolean {
  const c = typeof chord === "string" ? parseChord(chord) : chord;
  return (
    event.code === c.code &&
    event.ctrlKey === c.ctrl &&
    event.shiftKey === c.shift &&
    event.altKey === c.alt &&
    event.metaKey === c.meta
  );
}

/**
 * tinykeys pattern for the same chord. tinykeys compares the last token
 * against event.code as-is, so passing "KeyF" binds the physical key.
 */
export function chordToPattern(chord: string): string {
  const c = parseChord(chord);
  const mods: string[] = [];
  if (c.ctrl) mods.push("Control");
  if (c.alt) mods.push("Alt");
  if (c.shift) mods.push("Shift");
  if (c.meta) mods.push("Meta");
  return [...mods, c.code].join("+");
}
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { chordToPattern, matchesChord, type KeyLike } from "./chords";

function key(code: string, mods: Partial<KeyLike> = {}): KeyLike {
  return { code, ctrlKey: false, shiftKey: false, altKey: false, metaKey: false, ...mods };
}

describe("chordToPattern", () => {
  it("maps letters, digits and punctuation to physical codes", () => {
    expect(chordToPattern("Ctrl+Shift+F")).toBe("Control+Shift+KeyF");
    expect(chordToPattern("Ctrl+Alt+1")).toBe("Control+Alt+Digit1");
    expect(chordToPattern("Ctrl+\\")).toBe("Control+Backslash");
    expect(chordToPattern("Ctrl+/")).toBe("Control+Slash");
    expect(chordToPattern("Ctrl+[")).toBe("Control+BracketLeft");
    expect(chordToPattern("F11")).toBe("F11");
    expect(chordToPattern("Ctrl+Tab")).toBe("Control+Tab");
  });
});

describe("matchesChord", () => {
  it("matches by code, so a Russian layout still triggers Ctrl+B", () => {
    // Ctrl+B on a Russian layout: event.key is "��", event.code is "KeyB".
    expect(matchesChord("Ctrl+B", key("KeyB", { ctrlKey: true }))).toBe(true);
  });

  it("requires the modifiers to match exactly", () => {
    expect(matchesChord("Ctrl+F", key("KeyF", { ctrlKey: true }))).toBe(true);
    expect(matchesChord("Ctrl+F", key("KeyF", { ctrlKey: true, shiftKey: true }))).toBe(false);
    expect(matchesChord("Ctrl+F", key("KeyF"))).toBe(false);
    expect(matchesChord("F5", key("F5"))).toBe(true);
    expect(matchesChord("F5", key("F5", { altKey: true }))).toBe(false);
  });
});
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { DEFAULTS, parseSettings } from "./settings";

describe("parseSettings", () => {
  it("falls back to defaults on broken JSON and flags it", () => {
    const { settings, invalid } = parseSettings('{"appearance": {"theme": "dark",}');
    expect(invalid).toBe(true);
    expect(settings).toEqual(DEFAULTS);
  });

  it("keeps valid keys and clamps out-of-range ones", () => {
    const { settings, invalid } = parseSettings(
      JSON.stringify({
        appearance: { theme: "dark", fontSize: 99, contentWidth: 700 },
        edit: { lineNumbers: false, indentUnit: "tab" },
        library: { extensions: [".md", ".txt"] },
      }),
    );
    expect(invalid).toBe(false);
    expect(settings.appearance.theme).toBe("dark");
    expect(settings.appearance.fontSize).toBe(20);
    expect(settings.appearance.contentWidth).toBe(700);
    expect(settings.edit.lineNumbers).toBe(false);
    expect(settings.edit.indentUnit).toBe("tab");
    expect(settings.library.extensions).toEqual([".md", ".txt"]);
    // Untouched sections still come from the defaults.
    expect(settings.files.newFileEol).toBe(DEFAULTS.files.newFileEol);
  });

  it("replaces a bad value without discarding the rest of the file", () => {
    const { settings, invalid } = parseSettings(
      JSON.stringify({ appearance: { theme: "neon", contentWidth: 620 } }),
    );
    expect(invalid).toBe(false);
    expect(settings.appearance.theme).toBe("system");
    expect(settings.appearance.contentWidth).toBe(620);
  });
});

\u043f\u043e\u0441\u043b\u0435\u0434\u043e\u0432\u0430\u0442\u0435\u043b\u044c\u043d\u043e. \u041f\u043e \u0437\u0430\u0432\u0435\u0440\u0448\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0438 \u0443\u0441\u043f\u0435\u0448\u043d\u043e\u0439 \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u0438 `baseHash` **\u0432\u0441\u0435\u0433\u0434\u0430** \u043e\u0431\u043d\u043e\u0432\u043b\u044f\u0435\u0442\u0441\u044f \u0445\u044d\u0448\u0435\u043c \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u0430\u043d\u043d\u043e\u0433\u043e \u0441\u043d\u0438\u043c\u043a\u0430 (\u0438\u043d\u0430\u0447\u0435 \u0441\u043b\u0435\u0434\u0443\u044e\u0449\u0430\u044f \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u044c \u043f\u043e\u0439\u043c\u0430\u0435\u0442 \u043a\u043e\u043d\u0444\u043b\u0438\u043a\u0442 \u0441 \u0441\u043e\u0431\u0441\u0442\u0432\u0435\u043d\u043d\u044b\u043c \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b\u043e\u043c); \u0434\u0430\u043b\u0435\u0435 \u0444\u0440\u043e\u043d\u0442\u0435\u043d\u0434 \u0441\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043d\u0438\u0432\u0430\u0435\u0442 \u0442\u0435\u043a\u0443\u0449\u0438\u0439 \u0442\u0435\u043a\u0441\u0442 \u0441\u043e \u0441\u043d\u0438\u043c\u043a\u043e\u043c: \u0440\u0430\u0432\u0435\u043d \u2014 `saved`, \u0447\u0435\u0440\u043d\u043e\u0432\u0438\u043a \u0443\u0434\u0430\u043b\u0451\u043d; \u043d\u0435 \u0440\u0430\u0432\u0435\u043d \u2014 \u043e\u0441\u0442\u0430\u0451\u0442\u0441\u044f `unsaved`, \u0447\u0435\u0440\u043d\u043e\u0432\u0438\u043a \u043e\u0441\u0442\u0430\u0451\u0442\u0441\u044f (\u0441 \u043d\u043e\u0432\u044b\u043c `baseHash`). \u041e\u0448\u0438\u0431\u043a\u0430 \u2014 \u0431\u0430\u043d\u043d\u0435\u0440 `couldn't save \u2014 <\u043f\u0440\u0438\u0447\u0438\u043d\u0430> \u00b7 retry \u00b7 save as\u2026`. \u0420\u0435\u0436\u0438\u043c\u0430 in-place \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u0438 \u043d\u0435\u0442.\n- **`don't save`** \u043f\u0440\u0438 \u0437\u0430\u043a\u0440\u044b\u0442\u0438\u0438 \u2014 \u044f\u0432\u043d\u044b\u0439 \u043e\u0442\u043a\u0430\u0437 \u043e\u0442 \u043f\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043e\u043a: \u043e\u0436\u0438\u0434\u0430\u044e\u0449\u0430\u044f \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u044c \u0441\u043b\u0435\u0434\u0443\u044e\u0449\u0435\u043c \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0443\u0441\u043a\u0435 \u043d\u0438\u0447\u0435\u0433\u043e \u043d\u0435 \u0427\u0442\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0435: BOM \u2192 UTF-8 \u2192 `chardetng`. \u0417\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u044c: \u0432 \u043a\u043e\u0434\u0438\u0440\u043e\u0432\u043a\u0435 \u043f\u0435\u0440\u0432\u043e\u043c \u0441\u043e\u0445\u0440\u0430\u043d\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0438 \u043f\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043e\u043a `convert to UTF-8 \u00b7 save as\u2026 \u00b7 cancel`. EOL \u2014 \u0434\u043e\u043c\u0438\u043d\u0438\u0440\u0443\u044e\u0449\u0438\u0439 \u0441\u0442\u0438\u043b\u044c \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b\u0430 (\u043d\u043e\u0432\u044b\u0435 \u2014 \u0438\u0437 `files.newFileEol`, \u043f\u043e \u0443\u043c\u043e\u043b\u0447\u0430\u043d\u0438\u044e CRLF); \u0441\u043c\u0435\u0448\u0430\u043d\u043d\u044b\u0439 \u2192 \u043d\u043e\u0440\u043c\u0430\u043b\u0438\u0437\u0430\u0446\u0438\u044f \u0441 \u0441\u043e\u043e\u0431\u0449\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0435\u043c. `utf-8` \u0432 \u0441\u0442\u0430\u0442\u0443\u0441-\u0431\u0430\u0440\u0435 \u043a\u043b\u0438\u043a\u0430\u0431\u0435\u043b\u044c\u043d\u043e \u2192 `reopen as\u2026 (utf-8 \u00b7 cp1251 \u00b7 utf-16)`.\n- **\u0412\u043d\u0435\u0448\u043d\u0438\u0435 \u0438\u0437\u043c\u0435\u043d\u0435\u043d\u0438\u044f.** \u041d\u0435\u0442 \u043b\u043e\u043a\u0430\u043b\u044c\u043d\u044b\u0445 \u043f\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043e\u043a \u2192 \u0442\u0438\u0445\u0430\u044f \u043f\u0435\u0440\u0435\u0437\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430, \u0441\u043a\u0440\u043e\u043b\u043b \u043d\u0430 \u0442\u043e\u0442 \u0436\u0435 \u0437\u0430\u0433\u043e\u043b\u043e\u0432\u043e\u043a, \u0441\u0442\u0430\u0442\u0443\u0441 `reloaded from disk`, \u043e\u0434\u043d\u0430 \u0442\u043e\u0447\u043a\u0430 undo. \u0415\u0441\u0442\u044c \u043f\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043a\u0438 \u2192 \u0431\u0430\u043d\u043d\u0435\u0440 `file changed on disk \u00b7 reload \u00b7 save my copy as\u2026` (`reload` \u2014 \u0441 \u043f\u043e\u0434\u0442\u0432\u0435\u0440\u0436\u0434\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0435\u043c \u043f\u043e\u0442\u0435\u0440\u0438 \u043f\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043e\u043a; Rename \u0437\u0434\u0435\u0441\u044c \u2014 \u043f\u0440\u043e\u0441\u0442\u043e \u043f\u0435\u0440\u0435\u0438\u043c\u0435\u043d\u043e\u0432\u0430\u043d\u0438\u0435 \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b\u0430, \u0431\u0435\u0437 \u043f\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043a\u0438 \u0441\u0441\u044b\u043b\u043e\u043a.\n- **\u0412\u043a\u043b\u0430\u0434\u043a\u0438** \u2014 \u044d\u0442\u043e \u0441\u0435\u043a\u0446\u0438\u044f `open`; \u043e\u0442\u0434\u0435\u043b\u044c\u043d\u043e\u0439 \u043f\u043e\u043b\u043e\u0441\u044b \u0432\u043a\u043b\u0430\u0434\u043e\u043a \u043d\u0435\u0442. `Ctrl+Tab` \u2014 \u0446\u0438\u043a\u043b\u0438\u0447\u0435\u0441\u043a\u0438 \u043f\u043e \u0441\u043f\u0438\u0441\u043a\u0443, \u0431\u0435\u0437 \u0432\u0441\u043f\u043b\u044b\u0432\u0430\u044e\u0449\u0435\u0433\u043e \u043e\u043a\u043d\u0430.\n- **Quick search `Ctrl+K`** (\u043c\u0430\u043a\u0435\u0442 1f): \u043c\u043e\u0434\u0430\u043b `min(520px, 100% \u2212 32px)`, top 120px; \u043f\u0443\u0441\u0442\u043e\u0439 \u0432\u0432\u043e\u0434 \u2014 `recent`; \u0432\u0432\u043e\u0434 \u2014 \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b\u044b \u043f\u043e \u043f\u0443\u0442\u0438 (uFuzzy, \u0441\u043e\u0432\u043f\u0430\u0432\u0448\u0438\u0435 \u0441\u0438\u043c\u0432\u043e\u043b\u044b `--accent`); `>` \u2014 \u043a\u043e\u043c\u0430\u043d\u0434\u044b (cmdk). \u041f\u043e\u0434\u0432\u0430\u043b `\u2191\u2193 move \u00b7 \u21b5 open \u00b7 ctrl \u21b5 open in edit`.\n- **\u041f\u0443\u0441\u0442\u043e\u0435 \u0441\u043e\u0441\u0442\u043e\u044f\u043d\u0438\u0435**: `open a file \u00b7 open a library` \u0438 `recent`.\n- **\u0414\u0438\u0430\u043b\u043e\u0433\u0438** \u2014 \u043c\u043e\u0434\u0430\u043b 420px, \u0442\u0435\u043a\u0441\u0442\u043e\u0432\u044b\u0435 \u0434\u0435\u0439\u0441\u0442\u0432\u0438\u044f, `Esc` = \u043e\u0442\u043c\u0435\u043d\u0430. **\u0411\u0430\u043d\u043d\u0435\u0440\u044b** (\u043a\u043e\u043d\u0444\u043b\u0438\u043a\u0442, \u043e\u0448\u0438\u0431\u043a\u0430 \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u0438) \u2014 36px \u043f\u043e\u0434 \u043f\u043e\u043b\u043e\u0441\u043e\u0439 \u0440\u0435\u0436\u0438\u043c\u043e\u0432, `--surface`, \u043d\u0435 \u043a\u0440\u0430\u0434\u0443\u0442 \u0444\u043e\u043a\u0443\u0441. \u0422\u043e\u0441\u0442\u043e\u0432 \u043d\u0435\u0442.\n\n---\n\n## 5. \u0420\u0435\u0436\u0438\u043c\u044b\n\n**5.0. \u041e\u0431\u0449\u0435\u0435.** \u041e\u0434\u0438\u043d \u0431\u0443\u0444\u0435\u0440 \u043d\u0430 \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b \u2014 `EditorState` CodeMirror 6 \u0441\u043e \u0441\u0442\u0430\u043d\u0434\u0430\u0440\u0442\u043d\u043e\u0439 \u0438\u0441\u0442\u043e\u0440\u0438\u0435\u0439. \u043f\u0430\u043d\u0435\u043b\u0438 \u043f\u043e\u0434 \u043f\u043e\u043b\u043e\u0441\u043e\u0439 \u0440\u0435\u0436\u0438\u043c\u043e\u0432: \u0442\u0443\u043c\u0431\u043b\u0435\u0440\u044b-\u0433\u043b\u0438\u0444\u044b `Aa \u00b7 W \u00b7 .*`, \u0441\u0447\u0451\u0442\u0447\u0438\u043a, `Enter`/`Shift+Enter`, `Esc`. `replace all` \u2014 \u043e\u0434\u043d\u0430 \u0442\u0440\u0430\u043d\u0437\u0430\u043a\u0446\u0438\u044f undo.\n- \u0422\u0430\u0431\u043b\u0438\u0446\u044b, \u0430\u0432\u0442\u043e\u0434\u043e\u043f\u043e\u043b\u043d\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0435 \u043f\u0443\u0442\u0435\u0439/\u0442\u0435\u0433\u043e\u0432, \u043d\u0435\u0432\u0438\u0434\u0438\u043c\u044b\u0435 \u0441\u0438\u043c\u0432\u043e\u043b\u044b, \u0444\u043e\u0440\u043c\u0430\u0442\u0438\u0440\u043e\u0432\u0430\u043d\u0438\u0435 \u0442\u0430\u0431\u043b\u0438\u0446, \u043f\u043e\u0434\u0441\u043a\u0430\u0437\u043a\u0438 \u043f\u0440\u043e indented code, \u043e\u0440\u0444\u043e\u0433\u0440\u0430\u0444\u0438\u044f \u2014 **later**, \u043f\u043e \u043f\u043e\u0442\u0440\u0435\u0431\u043d\u043e\u0441\u0442\u0438.\n\n**5.3. Rich** `[v0.2, \u043a\u0430\u043d\u0434\u0438\u0434\u0430\u0442]` \u2014 \u0442\u043e\u0442 \u0436\u0435 `EditorState`, compartment \u0441 `Decoration.replace`, \u0441\u0435\u0440\u0438\u0430\u043b\u0438\u0437\u0430\u0446\u0438\u0438. \u0420\u0435\u0444\u0435\u0440\u0435\u043d\u0441 \u043a\u043e\u0434\u0430 \u2014 `@atomic-editor/editor`. \u0441\u043f\u0438\u0441\u043a\u043e\u043c \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b\u043e\u0432.\n- **\u041d\u0438\u043a\u0430\u043a\u043e\u0433\u043e \u0430\u0432\u0442\u043e\u0444\u043e\u0440\u043c\u0430\u0442\u0438\u0440\u043e\u0432\u0430\u043d\u0438\u044f** (\u00a71.3). \u0427\u0438\u0441\u0442\u044b\u0439 \u0434\u043e\u043a\u0443\u043c\u0435\u043d\u0442 \u043f\u0440\u0438 `Ctrl+S` \u043d\u0435 \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u044b\u0432\u0430\u0435\u0442\u0441\u044f.\n- **\u0410\u0442\u043e\u043c\u0430\u0440\u043d\u0430\u044f \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u044c.** \u0421\u043d\u0438\u043c\u043e\u043a \u0442\u0435\u043a\u0441\u0442\u0430 \u0431\u0443\u0444\u0435\u0440\u0430 + \u0435\u0433\u043e \u0432\u0435\u0440\u0441\u0438\u044f \u2192 Rust: temp-\u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b \u0432 \u0442\u043e\u0439 \u0436\u0435 \u043f\u0430\u043f\u043a\u0435 \u2192 `FlushFileBuffers` \u2192 `ReplaceFileW` (\u0441\u043e\u0445\u0440\u0430\u043d\u044f\u0435\u0442 creation time \u0438 \u0430\u0442\u0440\u0438\u0431\u0443\u0442\u044b); \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b\u0430 \u043d\u0435\u0442 \u2014 `MoveFileExW`. \u041f\u0435\u0440\u0435\u0434 \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u044c\u044e \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b \u043d\u0430 \u0434\u0438\u0441\u043a\u0435 \u0445\u044d\u0448\u0438\u0440\u0443\u0435\u0442\u0441\u044f \u0438 \u0441\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043d\u0438\u0432\u0430\u0435\u0442\u0441\u044f \u0441 `baseHash`: \u043d\u0435 \u0441\u043e\u0432\u043f\u0430\u043b \u2014 \u043d\u0435 \u043f\u0438\u0441\u0430\u0442\u044c, \u0431\u0430\u043d\u043d\u0435\u0440 \u043a\u043e\u043d\u0444\u043b\u0438\u043a\u0442\u0430. **\u0424\u0430\u0439\u043b \u043e\u0442\u0441\u0443\u0442\u0441\u0442\u0432\u0443\u0435\u0442** (\u0443\u0434\u0430\u043b\u0451\u043d \u0438\u0437\u0432\u043d\u0435) \u2014 \u043e\u0442\u0434\u0435\u043b\u044c\u043d\u044b\u0439 \u0441\u043b\u0443\u0447\u0430\u0439: \u0431\u0443\u0444\u0435\u0440 \u0441 \u043f\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043a\u0430\u043c\u0438 \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u044b\u0432\u0430\u0435\u0442\u0441\u044f \u0431\u0435\u0437 \u043f\u0440\u043e\u0432\u0435\u0440\u043a\u0438 \u0445\u044d\u0448\u0430, \u0441\u0442\u0430\u0442\u0443\u0441 `recreated`; \u0447\u0438\u0441\u0442\u044b\u0439 \u0431\u0443\u0444\u0435\u0440 \u0431\u0435\u0437 \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b\u0430 \u043d\u0438\u0447\u0435\u0433\u043e \u043d\u0435 \u043f\u0438\u0448\u0435\u0442. \u0417\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u0438 \u043e\u0434\u043d\u043e\u0433\u043e \u0434\u043e\u043a\u0443\u043c\u0435\u043d\u0442\u0430 \u0432\u044b\u043f\u043e\u043b\u043d\u044f\u044e\u0442\u0441\u044f \u043f\u043e\u0441\u043b\u0435\u0434\u043e\u0432\u0430\u0442\u0435\u043b\u044c\u043d\u043e. \u041f\u043e \u0437\u0430\u0432\u0435\u0440\u0448\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0438 \u0443\u0441\u043f\u0435\u0448\u043d\u043e\u0439 \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u0438 `baseHash` **\u0432\u0441\u0435\u0433\u0434\u0430** \u043e\u0431\u043d\u043e\u0432\u043b\u044f\u0435\u0442\u0441\u044f \u0445\u044d\u0448\u0435\u043c \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u0430\u043d\u043d\u043e\u0433\u043e \u0441\u043d\u0438\u043c\u043a\u0430 (\u0438\u043d\u0430\u0447\u0435 \u0441\u043b\u0435\u0434\u0443\u044e\u0449\u0430\u044f \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u044c \u043f\u043e\u0439\u043c\u0430\u0435\u0442 \u043a\u043e\u043d\u0444\u043b\u0438\u043a\u0442 \u0441 \u0441\u043e\u0431\u0441\u0442\u0432\u0435\u043d\u043d\u044b\u043c \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b\u043e\u043c); \u0434\u0430\u043b\u0435\u0435 \u0444\u0440\u043e\u043d\u0442\u0435\u043d\u0434 \u0441\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043d\u0438\u0432\u0430\u0435\u0442 \u0442\u0435\u043a\u0443\u0449\u0438\u0439 \u0442\u0435\u043a\u0441\u0442 \u0441\u043e \u0441\u043d\u0438\u043c\u043a\u043e\u043c: \u0440\u0430\u0432\u0435\u043d \u2014 `saved`, \u0447\u0435\u0440\u043d\u043e\u0432\u0438\u043a \u0443\u0434\u0430\u043b\u0451\u043d; \u043d\u0435 \u0440\u0430\u0432\u0435\u043d \u2014 \u043e\u0441\u0442\u0430\u0451\u0442\u0441\u044f `unsaved`, \u0447\u0435\u0440\u043d\u043e\u0432\u0438\u043a \u043e\u0441\u0442\u0430\u0451\u0442\u0441\u044f (\u0441 \u043d\u043e\u0432\u044b\u043c `baseHash`). \u041e\u0448\u0438\u0431\u043a\u0430 \u2014 \u0431\u0430\u043d\u043d\u0435\u0440 `couldn't save \u2014 <\u043f\u0440\u0438\u0447\u0438\u043d\u0430> \u00b7 retry \u00b7 save as\u2026`. \u0420\u0435\u0436\u0438\u043c\u0430 in-place \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u0438 \u043d\u0435\u0442.\n- **`don't save`** \u043f\u0440\u0438 \u0437\u0430\u043a\u0440\u044b\u0442\u0438\u0438 \u2014 \u044f\u0432\u043d\u044b\u0439 \u043e\u0442\u043c\u0435\u043d\u044f\u0435\u0442\u0441\u044f, \u0447\u0435\u0440\u043d\u043e\u0432\u0438\u043a \u0443\u0434\u0430\u043b\u044f\u0435\u0442\u0441\u044f, \u043f\u0440\u0438 \u0441\u043b\u0435\u0434\u0443\u044e\u0449\u0435\u043c \u0437\u0430\u043f\u0443\u0441\u043a\u0435 \u043d\u0438\u0447\u0435\u0433\u043e \u043d\u0435 \u0427\u0442\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0435: BOM \u2192 UTF-8 \u2192 `chardetng`. \u0417\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u044c: \u0432 \u043a\u043e\u0434\u0438\u0440\u043e\u0432\u043a\u0435 \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b\u0430, \u0435\u0441\u043b\u0438 \u044d\u0442\u043e UTF-8 \u0438\u043b\u0438 UTF-16 (BOM \u043a\u0430\u043a \u0431\u044b\u043b); legacy \u2192 \u043f\u0440\u0438 \u043f\u0435\u0440\u0432\u043e\u043c \u0441\u043e\u0445\u0440\u0430\u043d\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0438 \u043f\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043e\u043a `convert to UTF-8 \u00b7 save as\u2026 \u00b7 cancel`. EOL \u2014 \u0434\u043e\u043c\u0438\u043d\u0438\u0440\u0443\u044e\u0449\u0438\u0439 \u0441\u0442\u0438\u043b\u044c \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b\u0430 (\u043d\u043e\u0432\u044b\u0435 \u2014 \u0438\u0437 `files.newFileEol`, \u043f\u043e \u0443\u043c\u043e\u043b\u0447\u0430\u043d\u0438\u044e CRLF); \u0441\u043c\u0435\u0448\u0430\u043d\u043d\u044b\u0439 \u2192 \u043d\u043e\u0440\u043c\u0430\u043b\u0438\u0437\u0430\u0446\u0438\u044f \u0441 \u0441\u043e\u043e\u0431\u0449\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0435\u043c. `utf-8` \u0432 \u0441\u0442\u0430\u0442\u0443\u0441-\u0431\u0430\u0440\u0435 \u043a\u043b\u0438\u043a\u0430\u0431\u0435\u043b\u044c\u043d\u043e \u2192 `reopen as\u2026 (utf-8 \u00b7 cp1251 \u00b7 utf-16)`.\n- **\u0412\u043d\u0435\u0448\u043d\u0438\u0435 \u0438\u0437\u043c\u0435\u043d\u0435\u043d\u0438\u044f.** \u041d\u0435\u0442 \u043b\u043e\u043a\u0430\u043b\u044c\u043d\u044b\u0445 \u043f\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043e\u043a \u2192 \u0442\u0438\u0445\u0430\u044f \u043f\u0435\u0440\u0435\u0437\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430, \u0441\u043a\u0440\u043e\u043b\u043b \u043d\u0430 \u0442\u043e\u0442 \u0436\u0435 \u0437\u0430\u0433\u043e\u043b\u043e\u0432\u043e\u043a, \u0441\u0442\u0430\u0442\u0443\u0441 `reloaded from disk`, \u043e\u0434\u043d\u0430 \u0442\u043e\u0447\u043a\u0430 undo. \u0415\u0441\u0442\u044c \u043f\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043a\u0438 \u2192 \u0431\u0430\u043d\u043d\u0435\u0440 `file changed on disk \u00b7 reload \u00b7 save my copy as\u2026` (`reload` \u2014 \u0441 \u043f\u043e\u0434\u0442\u0432\u0435\u0440\u0436\u0434\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0435\u043c \u043f\u043e\u0442\u0435\u0440\u0438 \u043f\u0440\u0430\u0432\u043e\u043a; `[v0.1]`\n\n\u041b\u043e\u043a\u0430\u043b\u044c\u043d\u044b\u0435 \u043a\u0430\u0440\u0442\u0438\u043d\u043a\u0438 \u2014 Tauri asset protocol; scope \u0432\u044b\u0434\u0430\u0451\u0442\u0441\u044f (`../images/x.png` \u0443 \u043e\u0434\u0438\u043d\u043e\u0447\u043d\u043e\u0433\u043e \u0444\u0430\u0439\u043b\u0430) \u2192 \u043f\u043b\u0430\u0448\u043a\u0430 `outside folder \u00b7 allow` (\u0434\u043e\u0431\u0430\u0432\u043b\u044f\u0435\u0442 \u0435\u0451 \u043f\u0430\u043f\u043a\u0443 \u0432 scope \u043d\u0430 \u0441\u0435\u0441\u0441\u0438\u044e). \u0412\u043d\u0435\u0448\u043d\u0438\u0435 \u043a\u0430\u0440\u0442\u0438\u043d\u043a\u0438 \u2014 \u043f\u043b\u0430\u0448\u043a\u0430 \u0441 \u0445\u043e\u0441\u0442\u043e\u043c \u0438 `load`. \u0418\u0437\u043c\u0435\u043d\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0435 \u043a\u0430\u0440\u0442\u0438\u043d\u043a\u0438 \u043d\u0430 \u0434\u0438\u0441\u043a\u0435 \u043d\u0435 \u0412\u0441\u0442\u0430\u0432\u043a\u0430 \u043a\u0430\u0440\u0442\u0438\u043d\u043e\u043a \u0438\u0437 \u0431\u0443\u0444\u0435\u0440\u0430 \u0438 DnD \u0432 \u0442\u0435\u043a\u0441\u0442 \u2014 v0.2; \u043f\u043e\u043a\u0430 \u2014 \u0441\u043e\u0445\u0440\u0430\u043d\u0438\u0442\u044c PNG \u0432 `assets/` \u0440\u0443\u043a\u0430\u043c\u0438 \u0438 \u043d\u0430\u043f\u0438\u0441\u0430\u0442\u044c `![](assets/x.png)`.\n\n---\n\n## 10. \u041d\u0430\u0441\u0442\u0440\u043e\u0439\u043a\u0438 `[v0.1]`\n\n\u0424\u0430\u0439\u043b // Copyright 2019-2023 Tauri Programme within The Commons Conservancy
